## Heuristic / Inference Reanalysis And Validation
- Generated target name: `MotionAnimationTimerTick` is behavior-descriptive but not interface-accurate. TimerHandler's documented virtual contract and the secondary vtable slot make `MotionOnTimer` / `Motion::OnTimer` the best source-facing name.
- Callback signature: `retn 0Ch`, three stack arguments, `AL=1`, TimerHandler's accepted declaration, and adjusted vtable placement support a boolean three-argument override. The two unused trailing values remain named only in the class declaration and omitted in the method definition.
- Receiver identity: IDA presents callback `ECX` as the `TimerHandler` subobject. Owner at callback `+4` maps to primary Motion `+8`, and primary Motion is callback receiver minus four. This is normal non-primary-base virtual dispatch, not a manual source wrapper.
- Owner type: the callback unconditionally accesses `LivingObjectPane` offsets `+0x1bc`, `+0x1c0`, and `+0x1d1`; the primary constructor invokes ShowName/HideName on the same owner; the sole construction caller resides in LivingObjectPane action/effect handling. `LivingObjectPane *` is stronger than the existing alternate constructor's generic `ObjectPane *`.
- Frame fields: primary offsets `+0x12/+0x13/+0x14` are signed-character trigger/current/terminal fields, consistently written by both constructors and consumed here. Primary `+0x18` is the timer delay.
- Sound field and scaling: primary `+0x10` is a signed short sample/effect identifier with sentinel `-1`; the target sign-extends it for playback and performs unsigned four-fifths volume scaling. This is not a frame id or generic effect pointer.
- Owner list type and source API: both LivingObjectPane constructors allocate `List(4, 10)` into `+0x1bc`. Action creation appends the address of a primary `Motion *`; trimming and clearing retrieve pointer-sized element storage, cancel the object's secondary TimerHandler state, and invoke Motion's virtual scalar deletion; target completion retrieves the same primary type. The concrete source contract is therefore `List *` storing `Motion *` values, not `ObjectPane *` values and not a typed container. [UID:000079] List establishes `GetCount()`, `GetElementAt(index)`, `Append(count, source)`, `RemoveAt(index, count)`, and `RemoveAll()`; current `Count`, `At`, `Insert`, `Clear`, and boolean delete-flag arguments are stale pseudo-APIs.
- Current pointer: owner `+0x1c0` receives primary Motion `this` when rescheduled and is cleared on completion if any nonzero value exists. The action setter/clear paths and four render paths consume the same dword. `NewHumanImageLib::CalculateBounds` tests this pointer and reads signed byte `+0x13`, exactly Motion's current-frame field, independently ruling out `ObjectPane *`, sprite-config body-frame scalar, timer handle, and effect count.
- Name-visible byte: owner `+0x1d1` is set/cleared by documented ShowName/HideName behavior. Motion type 27 shows the name and intentionally holds at the final two-frame threshold; type 28 hides the name and continues. Generic suppression-state wording is superseded.
- Normal and nonnormal events: nonzero timer IDs return true without frame, sound, list, scheduling, cleanup, or map side effects. Timer ID zero performs all normal behavior.
- Completion: the callback removes itself if present, clears current effect independently of whether the list match was found, queues itself once through BlackHole's deduplicating helper, refreshes owner map bounds, and returns true. It does not explicitly invoke any destructor, free pool storage, or delete memory.
- Rescheduling: the callback schedules event ID zero with stored delay and zero payload values. Compiler conversion from Motion to its TimerHandler base explains the observed pointer adjustment and null-preserving compare. The ignored schedule return is not stored.
- Source lifetime: the construction caller allocates from the Motion pool, constructs, maintains the active-effect list, and schedules the timer. Completion removes the list reference and hands the primary LObject to BlackHole. The compiler scalar wrapper later destroys TimerHandler and LObject and conditionally frees through the Motion pool.
- Source placement: Motion RTTI names, both constructor vptr stores, timer slot, pool lifecycle, and current by-file route all agree on the existing Motion class/source bucket. Region rectangle helpers, MessageDialogs, NewHumanImageLib Motion.tbl support, and adjacent LivingObjectPane code remain separate.
- Cross-class access: protected access is impossible because Motion is not derived from LivingObjectPane; nested-class access is contradicted by top-level Motion RTTI; public raw fields are weaker than the class's encapsulated helper pattern; and accessor calls would invent call identities not required by the exact target body. The highest-probability mid-2000s source shape is one class-level Motion friendship in the authoritative complete LivingObjectPane declaration. Destination 9 supplies that single complete declaration now: `friend class Motion;` is in private scope beside `List *m_activeEffects`, `Motion *m_currentEffect`, and `unsigned char m_nameVisible`, so the direct Motion body is source-legal without a duplicate shell.
- Direct base and inherited views: decorated RTTI names only `LivingObjectPane`, and construction calls `ObjectPane(objectType=3, objectId, mapX, mapY)` before installing primary, EventHandler-adjusted, and TimerHandler-adjusted vtables at `+0/+0xa0/+0xa4`. Those secondary views are inherited through `ObjectPane -> Pane`; LivingObjectPane does not declare a second EventHandler or TimerHandler base. Its source-visible base list is exactly `public ObjectPane`.
- Exact base extension: ObjectPane ends at `+0x128`. The LivingObjectPane extension is `m_nameState` at `+0x128`, natural one-byte alignment, `wchar_t m_nameBuffer[33]` at `+0x12a`, constructor-written `m_nameStateToken` at `+0x16c`, two attached-object `List *` members at `+0x170/+0x174`, existing 68-byte `ObjectStatusBlob` at `+0x178`, `List *m_activeEffects` at `+0x1bc`, `Motion *m_currentEffect` at `+0x1c0`, animation/name/link/render state through `+0x1ec`, eight-byte nested BoxRotator at `+0x1f0`, render-effect dword at `+0x1f8`, and 16-byte RectBounds at `+0x1fc`; natural alignment ends exactly at `+0x20c`. The `+0x16c` dword is not filler invented to force size: both constructors explicitly clear it and bounded core/local searches found no other access, so the neutral state-token name records the evidence without assigning a false gameplay role.
- Existing value type: fixed 68-byte copies, MapPane parser callers, and current UID00009S establish `ObjectStatusBlob`, including `m_statusKind`, `m_appearanceId`, `m_weaponId`, and `m_displayStatusByte`. `LivingSpriteConfig`, `m_spriteConfig.kind/imageId/frameGroup/bodyFrame/palette/directionDelay`, and a second 68-byte class are rejected aliases. Animation group/frame/current Motion/box-rotation state are independent LivingObjectPane members at `+0x1cc/+0x1c8/+0x1c0/+0x1d4`.
- Nested helper: `BoxRotator@LivingObjectPane` decorated vtable data, constructor writes at `+0x1f0/+0x1f4`, delete size eight, FrameHandler teardown, and callback `0x0053d7c0` prove a private nested `BoxRotator : public FrameHandler` with one `LivingObjectPane *m_owner`. Destination 9 declares it once; UID00007C becomes declaration-covered support rather than emitting a second top-level class.
- Derived-only ownership: a standalone LivingObjectPane allocation is exactly `0x20c`, but bounded live functions `0x005a2de0`, `0x005a3870`, and `0x005a88d0` directly use `+0x13ead8/+0x13eadc`, compiler-adjusted one-based inventory addressing `+0x133f08 + slot*0x1fc` whose actual 52-record array begins at `+0x134104`, and `+0x1340f8/+0x1340cc..+0x1340e8`. UserPane construction first invokes the LivingObjectPane base constructor, allocates exactly `0x13eb84`, and initializes this large tail. Therefore the current local-player bodies are source-authored UserPane members viewed from the primary base, not legal base members and not non-reconstructable code.
- UserPane layout closure: `LinkedPlayerRecord[50]` occupies exact `+0x218..+0x3cb0` with `0x12c` stride and count byte at `+0x3cb0`; local-player hit radius is `+0x3ec4`; existing `CollectionPlayerDataView` occupies `+0x3ec8..+0x1340ca` followed by natural two-byte alignment; screen center/bounds and `MapPane *m_mapPane` occupy `+0x1340cc..+0x1340fc`; 52 one-based inventory records occupy `+0x134104..+0x13a834`; 52 spell-command records immediately occupy `+0x13a834..+0x13ead4`; destructor-proven List pointers occupy `+0x13eb04/+0x13eb08/+0x13eb0c`; and exact movement/action/history/deferred-packet fields continue through `+0x13eb3c`, followed by evidenced but semantically neutral tail bytes to allocation end `+0x13eb84`. Destination 12 represents this as one authoritative derived declaration, not a second class shell or a partial field list.
- Sibling source-family audit: every modeled receiver-bearing start from `0x005a2de0` through `0x005ad360` either directly accesses a UserPane-only displacement or is called with the `UserPane` receiver from UserPane virtuals. Direct xrefs prove the live movement, hotkey, inventory/spell, action, warp, history, screen, send, and server-packet methods. Route-negative UID0003UA/UID0003VE/UID0003UJ/UID0003UO/UID0003UV/UID0003UX/UID0003UY/UID0003H5 remain retained source-shaped UserPane helpers; UID0003UO/UID0003US and UID0003UX/UID0003UY/UID0003H5 have named live inline-equivalent consumers but retain their independently present raw bodies unless a later compiler-identical one-definition pass proves covered-by emission is required. UID0003UT is `__stdcall` and UID0003H4 reads no receiver, so they are file-local/static UserPane.cpp helpers. UID0003UU is outside the receiver contradiction and remains unchanged.
- Raw helper source shape: `0x0053b060`, `0x0053b0e0`, and `0x0053b190` decode as complete typed add/remove/set helpers but have no IDA function object and no inbound xrefs. They remain retained source-shaped bodies in UID0003Z5, corrected to `Motion *` and the real List API; their no-route status does not justify `ObjectPane *` or pseudo-List declarations. The action method's duplicate sequence remains an observed inline expansion, not evidence of a direct call.
- Generated order: class declaration before static storage and definitions is required for compilable output; constructor and callback definitions follow in address/source family order; compiler markers follow source methods.
- Rejected alternatives:
  - `Motion::Tick` or `Motion::OnTimerTick`: weaker than inherited `TimerHandler::OnTimer` identity.
  - free callback or TimerMgr-owned helper: contradicted by adjusted Motion vtable slot and member state.
  - `ObjectPane *` owner: too broad for unconditional LivingObjectPane-only offsets and name methods.
  - `ObjectPane *` active-list/current entries: impossible because stored objects have Motion RTTI/layout, are adjusted by `+4` for TimerHandler cleanup, and are deleted through Motion's scalar wrapper; Motion is not an ObjectPane.
  - public LivingObjectPane raw fields: source-compilable but less likely than a narrow friend token given the class's existing helper/encapsulation pattern.
  - protected LivingObjectPane fields: cannot grant access to unrelated Motion.
  - nested Motion: contradicted by top-level Motion RTTI and separate pool/class route.
  - invented public accessor API: possible after inlining but adds names and declarations without positive call or historical documentation evidence; class-level friendship explains the exact direct source shape with fewer assumptions.
  - timer handle at owner `+0x1c0`: contradicted by ignored schedule return and stored primary Motion pointer.
  - `m_suppressLastFrames`: weaker than direct ShowName/HideName producer evidence for `m_nameVisible`.
  - explicit vptr writes/base destructor/delete code: compiler output, not human target body.
  - split target or retain raw helper: one modeled source function with an inherited virtual route.
- Bounded original-name uncertainty: no source filename string, local Motion UDT, enum symbols, exact private field spellings, or physical header filename survives. Current evidence nevertheless fixes the concrete pointer types, List API, complete class/base/nested layout, ObjectStatusBlob integration, friend access token, and base-versus-derived emission boundary. Neutral names on constructor-written but otherwise unread `+0x128/+0x16c` state cap confidence; they do not defer a declaration, access, type, layout, or ownership decision.

## Evidence Standards Used
- Evidence types: fresh IDA MCP discovery/health, bounded raw bytes, function lookup/analyze/decompile/disassembly, exact range hash, basic-block/complexity data, xrefs, vtable/RTTI dwords and constructor stores, callers/callees, helper decompiles, `List(4,10)` construction and exact List declaration, Motion scalar-wrapper/TimerHandler cleanup, NewHuman pointer consumers, adjacent boundaries, current by-* docs, generated source, manual coverage, historical reports, old generated leads, and negative searches.
- Strength: target control flow and data roles are jointly proven by direct instructions and independent producers/consumers. Multiple inheritance is proven by two constructor vptr stores and adjusted table layout. Source placement is corroborated by RTTI, pool, caller, class/file docs, and generated route.
- Evidence ladder: live evidence-time MCP binary facts outrank historical decompiler output; accepted docs supply semantic names only where their own evidence agrees; stripped-name source spellings are explicitly inference. No fallback-only fact is used.
- Tool limitations: an early client-side SSE parser interpreted an initialization notification as tool content; an early client extracted the wrong session property and received `database required`; two early `get_bytes` attempts used `address/size` and then `start/size` rather than the live schema's `regions:{addr,size}`, yielding request-shape errors. A later local PowerShell syntax/IE-parser issue occurred before an IDB call. B001 inspected `tools/list`, corrected the schema, and valid worker-backed calls succeeded. These are transport/client limitations, not evidence of IDB failure. The later real listener outage caused an immediate pause; all MCP-dependent conclusions were revalidated after supervisor recovery.

## Positive Evidence Summary
- Direct target facts remain unchanged: exact function range/hash, target vtable xref, secondary receiver adjustment, five callees, all branches, signed packed fields, owner offsets, scheduler arguments, deferred cleanup, common map refresh, and true-return ABI.
- Concrete container/type facts now agree end to end: constructors create `List(4,10)`; action appends primary Motion pointer bytes; raw trim/remove/set and clear operate on that type; target scans/removes/stores it; TimerHandler cleanup uses Motion `+4`; scalar deletion tears down Motion; four NewHuman consumers pass the same current Motion pointer and read its frame byte.
- Exact source API facts come from the accepted complete List declaration and observed slots: `GetCount`, dereferenced `GetElementAt`, `Append(count, &pointer)`, `RemoveAt(index,count)`, and `RemoveAll`.
- The access inference is bounded and explicit: top-level RTTI and separate inheritance reject nested/protected routes, while class-level Motion friendship makes the exact direct source legal without exposing raw fields or inventing accessors. The broad LivingObjectPane class remains one declaration and is not replaced by a partial shell.
- Strongest combined chain: constructor vptrs/RTTI establish Motion plus secondary TimerHandler; action/clear/render consumers independently establish the owner storage types; target receiver-relative fields map exactly to constructor-written Motion fields; friendship supplies the source access token; completion reverses list/current ownership and queues the primary object. This supports a behavior-complete, type-consistent `Motion::OnTimer` source body.
- Independent derived-owner chain: UserPane pool size `0x13eb84`, constructor-after-base layout, exact one-based inventory/spell arrays, MapPane `+0x418` back-pointer, UserPane virtual callers, and every large receiver displacement jointly prove source-authored UserPane membership. Zero-xref raw starts remain source-shaped because they contain packet/config/history behavior and share exact derived fields with live members; no raw start has compiler-thunk, vtable, EH-only, or padding characteristics.
- Semantic-name/body chain: complete list lifetime behavior separates attachment-only clear from full linked-object teardown; Motion pointer operations separate add/remove/current-state helpers; owner invalidation separates the public linked-mode virtual from its private traversal; dispatcher opcodes `0x0b` and `0x26` plus distinct branches separate baseline and extended movement replies; and the `0x1fc` inventory stride rejects the spell parser label. Those facts support all 23 added formal destinations without reverse-engineering identifiers.

- Data/table/padding facts: four CC bytes immediately before and after the function; predecessor constructor-local table ends before the pre-padding; successor function begins at `0x0053a110`; no table or embedded data exists inside target.

- Vtable/global/type facts:

- Negative IDA facts: no strings/constants in target, no source path, no direct caller, no target-specific standalone pointer, no scheduler-result store, and no explicit destructor/free/delete.
- Repair type facts: constructors `0x0053a110`/`0x0053a360` create `List(4,10)` at `+0x1bc`; action `0x0053b3d0` appends constructed Motion; clear `0x0053b130` destroys stored Motion values; raw starts `0x0053b060`, `0x0053b0e0`, `0x0053b190` decode but have no function objects/xrefs; NewHuman consumers at `0x0053a700`, `0x0053bb20`, `0x0053bd20`, and `0x0053bdc0` pass `+0x1c0`; callee `0x004e09e0` reads pointer byte `+0x13`.
- UserPane facts: fresh lookup found 26 modeled functions and nine raw helper starts across the exact UID0001KM family; fresh xrefs independently separate live methods from retained raw bodies. Modeled decompiles establish UserPane receiver state throughout. `0x005abda0` is `__stdcall` with packet-only input; `0x005acae0` has stack direction input and no `this`; both route as file-local/static UserPane.cpp helpers rather than methods.
- Semantic/body facts: dispatcher call sites `0x005a7a33` and `0x005a7a44` bind opcodes `0x0b` and `0x26` to the baseline and extended movement-reply bodies. UID0003UM writes the inventory table, not the spell table. All 21 formerly blank pages contain ordinary source behavior; no one is an adjustor, EH-only wrapper, vtable/data island, or padding range. The formal-region lexical scan contains zero reverse-engineering placeholder identifiers.

- Current UserPane repair-triggering docs: UID0000FQ has only a declaration shell; UID0001KM and 35 affected exact child pages misroute UserPane members or UserPane.cpp helpers through LivingObjectPane; fourteen populated formal blocks therefore emit or propose uncompilable base-qualified definitions, while 21 reconstructable/source-bearing pages remain blank. Existing executed reports preserve much of the behavior, but their former owner inference predated the exact `0x20c` base versus `0x13eb84` derived layout closure. Destinations 12-26 and 29-49 now supply one complete declaration and all 35 source bodies. UID0003UU is the separately proven packet-only LivingObjectPane.cpp static helper and is not part of the contradiction.

The later applied source ordering and formal blocks removed the class/target empties while keeping the scalar-wrapper compiler disposition marker.
- The historical unrefined Motion.cpp explicitly exhibits the timer-view `this - 4` pattern but also contains unrelated polluted families. It is corroboration only and is superseded wherever the live evidence pass and current ownership docs are more precise.

## Ranked Ownership Analysis

### 1. UID00008R Motion under UID0000LL Motion.cpp
- Evidence for: exact Motion RTTI/vtables, both Motion constructors, secondary TimerHandler slot, Motion pool size/lifecycle, LivingObjectPane construction caller, target member layout, and current generated route.
- Evidence against: exact original physical filename and member spellings are absent from the stripped binary.
- Decision: accepted direct owner/emitter and source root; residual spelling uncertainty caps confidence but does not block source.

### 2. LivingObjectPane source family
- Evidence for: owner fields, allocation caller, active-effect list, current-effect pointer, name visibility, and map refresh all operate on LivingObjectPane.
- Evidence against: receiver is a separate 0x1c Motion allocation with Motion RTTI/vptrs/pool and TimerHandler base; target is not a LivingObjectPane method.
- Decision: bounded support dependency and owner type, rejected as target source owner.

### 3. TimerMgr / TimerHandler, SoundManager, MapPane, or BlackHole
- Evidence for: target implements a TimerHandler virtual and calls each manager/helper.
- Evidence against: dependency calls do not transfer ownership; Motion vtable and fields uniquely own the body.
- Decision: interface/dependency support only; rejected as direct owner.

### 4. UID0000FQ UserPane under UID0000P1 UserPane.cpp
- Evidence for: exact `0x13eb84` allocation, constructor after LivingObjectPane base construction, UserPane RTTI/vtables, existing class/file route, UserPane singleton/global, MapPane `+0x418` back-pointer, direct UserPane virtual callers, and all receiver-only offsets above `0x20c`.

- Decision: direct owner/emitter for every receiver-bearing local-player function. Route-negative bodies remain retained source and confidence-capped. No-receiver UID0003UT/UID0003H4 use file-level UID0000P1 instead of class UID0000FQ.

### 5. UID00007B LivingObjectPane as local-player source owner

- Evidence against: standalone allocation ends at `0x20c`; required local-player fields extend to `0x13eb3c`; UserPane-specific callers and vtable/source cluster dominate.

## Negative Evidence Summary
- No direct code caller exists, which is expected for vtable dispatch and does not imply dead/raw status.
- No target string, source filename, debug UDT, enum symbol, or original member names survive.
- No manager/global consumer establishes manager ownership; all are ordinary dependencies.
- No scheduler return reaches owner `+0x1c0`; timer-handle wording is false.
- No list deletion flag is passed; RemoveAt receives count one. Deferred destruction happens through BlackHole, not during list removal.
- No explicit destructor, pool free, delete, vptr store, base teardown, EH wrapper, or adjusted thunk belongs in the target body.
- No adjacent Region, MessageDialogs, NewHumanImageLib, or object-pane function lies inside the exact target range.
- No evidence supports a new source file, target split, target raw-helper reclassification, or target no-code disposition. The separate UID0003Z5 no-xref bodies remain retained support evidence.
- Motion has no ObjectPane inheritance or conversion, so `ObjectPane *` cannot be the common list/current type.
- No List declaration supports `Count`, `At`, `Insert`, `Clear`, or boolean delete-on-remove; those tokens are reconstruction artifacts.
- No protected route can cross unrelated classes, and top-level Motion RTTI rejects nesting.
- No positive call identity supports invented LivingObjectPane accessors. Class friendship is therefore preferred to accessor speculation; its exact original declaration location remains non-binary source syntax and is confidence-capped.

bool Motion::OnTimer(int timerId, int, int)
{
    if (timerId != 0)
        return true;

    if (m_owner->m_nameVisible != 1 ||
        m_currentFrame < m_terminalFrame - 2)
    {
        ++m_currentFrame;
    }

    if (m_soundEffectId != -1 &&
        m_currentFrame == m_soundTriggerFrame)
    {
        g_pSoundManager->PlaySample(
            m_soundEffectId,
            (4U * g_pSoundManager->GetSampleVolume()) / 5U);
    }

    if (m_currentFrame < m_terminalFrame)
    {
        g_pTimerMgr->ScheduleTimer(this, 0, m_timerDelay, 0, 0);
        if (m_owner->m_currentEffect != this)
            m_owner->m_currentEffect = this;
    }
    else
    {
        const int effectCount = m_owner->m_activeEffects->GetCount();
        for (int i = 0; i < effectCount; ++i)
        {
            Motion *effect = *static_cast<Motion **>(
                m_owner->m_activeEffects->GetElementAt(i));
            if (effect == this)
            {
                m_owner->m_activeEffects->RemoveAt(i, 1);
                break;
            }
        }

        if (m_owner->m_currentEffect != 0)
            m_owner->m_currentEffect = 0;

        g_pApplicationCleanupQueue->EnqueueIfMissing(this);
    }

    g_activeMapPane->UpdateObjectPaneScreenBounds(m_owner);
    return true;
}

Motion::Motion(LivingObjectPane *owner, int motionType, char terminalFrame, int timerDelay, char soundOverride)
    : LObject(),
      TimerHandler()
{
    m_owner = owner;
    m_timerDelay = timerDelay;
    m_terminalFrame = terminalFrame;

    char requestedSound = -1;
    if (soundOverride != 0)
        requestedSound = soundOverride;

    m_motionType = motionType;
    m_currentFrame = -1;

    switch (motionType)
    {
    case 1:
    case 2:
        m_soundTriggerFrame = 1;
        break;
    case 27:
    case 28:
        break;
    default:
        m_soundTriggerFrame = 0;
        break;
    }

    if (requestedSound < 0)
    {
        m_soundEffectId = -1;
        return;
    }

    switch (motionType)
    {
    case 2:
        m_soundEffectId = 709;
        break;
    case 4:
        m_soundEffectId = 406;
        break;
    case 8:
        m_soundEffectId = 403;
        break;
    case 9:
        m_soundEffectId = 312;
        break;
    case 10:
        m_soundEffectId = 313;
        break;
    case 11:
        m_soundEffectId = 300;
        break;
    case 12:
        m_soundEffectId = 301;
        break;
    case 13:
        m_soundEffectId = 302;
        break;
    case 14:
        m_soundEffectId = 303;
        break;
    case 15:
        m_soundEffectId = 304;
        break;
    case 16:
        m_soundEffectId = (g_pConfig->m_motionType16SoundEnabled == 1) ? 305 : -1;
        break;
    case 17:
        m_soundEffectId = 306;
        break;
    case 18:
        m_soundEffectId = 307;
        break;
    case 19:
        m_soundEffectId = 308;

    case 20:
        m_soundEffectId = 309;
        break;
    case 21:
        m_soundEffectId = 310;
        break;
    case 22:
        m_soundEffectId = 311;
        break;
    case 23:
        m_soundEffectId = 314;
        break;
    case 24:
        m_soundEffectId = 315;
        break;
    default:
        m_soundEffectId = requestedSound;
        break;
    }
}

void LivingObjectPane::AddMotion(Motion *effect)
{
    while (m_activeEffects->GetCount() > 2)
    {
        const int index = m_activeEffects->GetCount() - 3;
        Motion *oldEffect = *static_cast<Motion **>(
            m_activeEffects->GetElementAt(index));
        if (oldEffect != 0)
        {
            oldEffect->RemovePendingTimers();
            delete oldEffect;
        }
        m_activeEffects->RemoveAt(index, 1);
        if (m_currentEffect == oldEffect)
            m_currentEffect = 0;
    }

    m_activeEffects->Append(1, &effect);
}

void LivingObjectPane::RemoveMotion(Motion *effect)
{
    const int effectCount = m_activeEffects->GetCount();
    for (int i = 0; i < effectCount; ++i)
    {
        Motion *storedEffect = *static_cast<Motion **>(
            m_activeEffects->GetElementAt(i));
        if (storedEffect == effect)
        {
            m_activeEffects->RemoveAt(i, 1);
            break;
        }
    }
}

void LivingObjectPane::ClearAllEffects()
{
    const int effectCount = m_activeEffects->GetCount();
    for (int i = 0; i < effectCount; ++i)
    {
        Motion *effect = *static_cast<Motion **>(
            m_activeEffects->GetElementAt(i));
        delete effect;
    }
    m_activeEffects->RemoveAll();
    m_renderEffectState = 0;
    m_currentEffect = 0;
}

void LivingObjectPane::SetCurrentMotion(Motion *effect)
{
    if (m_currentEffect != effect)
        m_currentEffect = effect;
}

void LivingObjectPane::SetHighlightState(bool highlighted)
{
    if (m_highlighted == highlighted)
        return;

    m_highlighted = highlighted;
    if (highlighted)
        m_boxRotator.Start();
    else
        m_boxRotator.Stop();
    Invalidate(false);
}

void LivingObjectPane::ShowDamageEffect(int value, short soundId)
{
    g_pSoundManager->PlaySample(soundId, 100);
    if (m_objectStatus.m_statusKind == ObjectStatusBlob::MonsterObject &&
        m_objectStatus.m_appearanceId > 0x8173 &&
        m_objectStatus.m_appearanceId < 0x8187)
        return;

    m_renderEffectState = value;
    Invalidate(&m_bounds);
    g_pTimerMgr->ScheduleTimer(
        static_cast<TimerHandler *>(this),
        kLivingTimerRefresh, 120, 0, 0);
}

bool LivingObjectPane::BeginMoveTo(int x, int y, unsigned char facing, short stepDelay)
{
    m_facing = facing;

    if (m_isMoving)
    {
        CancelTimer(kLivingTimerForwardStep);
        if (TryStepForward())
            return true;

        if (m_highlighted)
        {
            m_highlighted = true;
            m_boxRotator.Start();
            Invalidate(false);
        }

        m_renderEffectState = 0;
    }

    m_facing = facing;
    if (m_mapX != x || m_mapY != y)
        SetMapPosition(x, y);

    m_isMoving = true;
    m_moveFrame = 0;
    g_pTimerMgr->ScheduleTimer(
        static_cast<TimerHandler *>(this),
        kLivingTimerForwardStep, stepDelay, facing, stepDelay);
    return g_pActiveMapPane->InvalidateObject(this);
}

void LivingObjectPane::SetFacing(unsigned char facing)
{
    if (m_isMoving)
    {
        g_pTimerMgr->ScheduleTimer(
            static_cast<TimerHandler *>(this),
            kLivingTimerDirectionDelay,
            m_objectStatus.m_displayStatusByte,
            facing, 0);
        return;
    }

    m_facing = facing;
    if (m_objectStatus.m_statusKind == ObjectStatusBlob::MonsterObject)
        PlayAnimation(facing + 1);
    g_pActiveMapPane->InvalidateObject(this);
}

void LivingObjectPane::PlayActionAnimationOrEffect(unsigned action, int value, unsigned char facing)
{
    if (m_objectStatus.m_statusKind == ObjectStatusBlob::MonsterObject)
    {
        if (m_animationGroup != 0 && m_animationGroup <= 8)
        {
            const bool canPlayActionGroup = (m_nameBuffer[0] == L'\0');
            switch (action)
            {
            case 0:  PlayAnimation(m_facing + 5); break;
            case 1:  PlayAnimation(m_facing + 13); break;
            case 2:  if (canPlayActionGroup) PlayAnimation(m_facing + 17); break;
            case 3:  if (canPlayActionGroup) PlayAnimation(m_facing + 21); break;
            case 4:  if (canPlayActionGroup) PlayAnimation(m_facing + 25); break;
            case 5:  if (canPlayActionGroup) PlayAnimation(m_facing + 29); break;
            case 6:  return;
            case 25: PlayAnimation(m_facing + 9); break;
            default: if (canPlayActionGroup) PlayAnimation(4); break;
            }
        }
        return;
    }

    if (action == 25)
        return;
    if (m_nameVisible && action != 27 && action != 4 && action != 6)
        return;
    if (action == 27)
        ClearAllEffects();

    Motion *effect = 0;
    void *storage = g_motionPool.Allocate();
    if (storage != 0)
        effect = new (storage) Motion(this, action, static_cast<short>(value), facing);

    while (m_activeEffects->GetCount() > 2)
    {
        const int index = m_activeEffects->GetCount() - 3;
        Motion *oldEffect = *static_cast<Motion **>(
            m_activeEffects->GetElementAt(index));
        if (oldEffect != 0)
        {
            oldEffect->RemovePendingTimers();
            delete oldEffect;
        }
        m_activeEffects->RemoveAt(index, 1);
        if (m_currentEffect == oldEffect)
            m_currentEffect = 0;
    }

    m_activeEffects->Append(1, &effect);
    g_pTimerMgr->ScheduleTimer(
        effect != 0 ? static_cast<TimerHandler *>(effect) : 0,
        0, 0, 0, 0);
}

unsigned char RemapMovementDirectionForMapScroll(unsigned char direction)
{
    switch (direction)
    {
    case 1:
        return 3;
    case 2:
        return 0;
    case 3:

// 0x0053a6f0-0x0053a6fb is a no-function/no-xref code-shaped island
// inside the switch-table/padding gap; it is documented but not emitted as
// a callable source helper until a caller or source route is proven.

void LivingObjectPane::GetRenderBounds(NxRect *imageRect, NxRect *screenRect) const
{
    if (g_activeMapPane == 0)
        return;

    if (m_objectStatus.m_statusKind != ObjectStatusBlob::HumanObject)
    {
        if (g_activeMapPane->IsHighResolutionMode())
        {
            if (m_animationGroup < 9 && m_isMoving)
                g_pMonsterImageLib->GetFrameBounds(m_objectStatus, m_facing + 5, m_moveFrame, imageRect, &m_bounds);
            else
                g_pMonsterImageLib->GetFrameBounds(m_objectStatus, m_animationGroup, m_animationFrame, imageRect, &m_bounds);

            OffsetBoundsByObjectAnchor(imageRect, m_bounds);
        }
        else
        {
            g_pMonsterImageLib->GetFrameBounds(m_objectStatus, m_animationGroup, m_animationFrame, imageRect, 0);
        }
    }
    else
    {
        g_pNewHumanImageLib->GetFrameBounds(m_objectStatus,
                                            m_facing,
                                            m_currentEffect,
                                            m_moveFrame,
                                            imageRect,
                                            g_activeMapPane->IsHighResolutionMode() ? &m_bounds : 0,
                                            0,
                                            m_isMoving,
                                            m_boxRotationStep);

        if (g_activeMapPane->IsHighResolutionMode())
            OffsetBoundsByObjectAnchor(imageRect, m_bounds);
    }

    g_activeMapPane->BuildObjectScreenRect(this, imageRect, screenRect);
}

void LivingObjectPane::GetSpriteConfig(ObjectStatusBlob *out) const
{
    *out = m_objectStatus;
}

bool LivingObjectPane::SetSpriteConfig(const ObjectStatusBlob *config, unsigned char facing)
{
    if (m_objectStatus.m_statusKind != config->m_statusKind)
    {
        m_objectStatus = *config;

        if (m_isMoving && TryStepForward())
            return true;
    }

    CancelPendingTimersExceptEvent(kLivingTimerRefresh);
    m_moveFrame = 0;

    if (config->m_statusKind == ObjectStatusBlob::MonsterObject)
        PlayAnimation(facing + 1);

    m_objectStatus = *config;
    m_facing = facing;
    CancelTimer(kLivingTimerDirectionDelay);

    HideName();
    return g_activeMapPane->InvalidateObject(this);
}

void LivingObjectPane::InitializeSpriteConfig(const ObjectStatusBlob *config, unsigned char facing)
{
    m_objectStatus = *config;
    m_facing = facing;
    m_currentEffect = 0;
    m_isMoving = false;
    m_moveFrame = 0;
    m_animationFrame = 0;
    m_terminalAnimation = false;
    m_highlighted = false;

    HideName();

    m_animationGroup = facing + 1;

    if (m_objectStatus.m_statusKind == ObjectStatusBlob::MonsterObject)
    {
        const int delay = g_pMonsterImageLib->GetFrameDelay(m_objectStatus.m_appearanceId - 0x8000,
                                                           m_animationGroup,
                                                           m_animationFrame);
        g_pTimerMgr->ScheduleTimer(
            static_cast<TimerHandler *>(this),
            kLivingTimerFrameAnimation, delay, 0, 0);
    }

    m_nameState = 0;
    m_boxRotationPhase = 0;
    m_boxRotator.m_owner = this;
}

void LivingObjectPane::RenderFrame(GrafPort *target, const RectBounds *bounds)
{
    FrameDrawRecord *translatedClipRecord = 0;
    float translatedClipOffset = 0.0f;
    const bool useMapLightOverlay = g_pActiveMapPane->ShouldUseLivingObjectLightOverlay();

    if (m_elevationOffset != 0.0f)
    {
        if (g_pActiveMapPane->ShouldOffsetElevatedLivingObjects())
        {
            translatedClipRecord = target->m_translatedClipRecord;
            translatedClipOffset = target->m_translatedClipOffsetY + m_elevationOffset;
        }
    }

    if (m_objectStatus.m_statusKind != ObjectStatusBlob::HumanObject)
    {
        if (m_animationGroup < 9 && m_isMoving)
        {
            g_pMonsterImageLib->RenderMonsterImage(
                target,
                bounds,
                &m_objectStatus,
                m_facing + 5,
                m_renderEffectState,
                m_moveFrame,
                useMapLightOverlay,
                translatedClipRecord,
                translatedClipOffset);
        }
        else
        {
            g_pMonsterImageLib->RenderMonsterImage(
                target,
                bounds,
                &m_objectStatus,
                m_animationGroup,
                m_renderEffectState,
                m_animationFrame,
                useMapLightOverlay,
                translatedClipRecord,
                translatedClipOffset);
        }
    }
    else
    {
        g_pNewHumanImageLib->Draw(
            target,
            bounds,
            &m_objectStatus,
            m_facing,
            m_currentEffect,
            m_renderEffectState,
            m_moveFrame,
            useMapLightOverlay,
            translatedClipRecord,
            translatedClipOffset,
            0,
            m_isMoving,
            m_boxRotationStep);
    }

    if (m_markerOverlayEnabled)
    {
        const unsigned int *colors = kLivingObjectMarkerFrameColors + m_markerOverlayIndex;

        if (g_pActiveMapPane->UsesAlternateLivingObjectMarkerColors())
        {
            const int colorSet = g_pActiveMapPane->GetLivingObjectMarkerColorSet();
            if (colorSet == 0)
                colors = kLivingObjectMarkerColorSet0 + m_markerOverlayIndex;
            else if (colorSet == 1)
                colors = kLivingObjectMarkerColorSet1 + m_markerOverlayIndex;
        }

        target->m_drawMode = 0;

        RectBounds markerBounds = *bounds;
        target->SetDrawColor(colors[0]);
        target->DrawRectFrame(&markerBounds);

        for (int i = 1; i < 4; ++i)
        {
            markerBounds.Inset(1, 1);
            target->SetDrawColor(colors[i]);
            target->DrawRectFrame(&markerBounds);
        }
    }
}

void LivingObjectPane::DrawSpriteFrame(GrafPort *target, const RectBounds *bounds)
{
    if (m_objectStatus.m_statusKind != ObjectStatusBlob::HumanObject)
    {
        if (m_animationGroup < 9 && m_isMoving)
        {
            g_pMonsterImageLib->RenderMonster(
                target,
                bounds,
                &m_objectStatus,
                m_facing + 5,
                m_moveFrame);
        }
        else
        {
            g_pMonsterImageLib->RenderMonster(
                target,
                bounds,
                &m_objectStatus,
                m_animationGroup,
                m_animationFrame);
        }
        return;
    }

    g_pNewHumanImageLib->RenderHuman(
        target,
        bounds,
        &m_objectStatus,
        m_facing,
        m_currentEffect,
        m_moveFrame,
        m_isMoving,
        m_boxRotationStep);
}

*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:[[[]]] | ONLY MODIFY VALUE - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:BEGIN | ONLY MODIFY BETWEEN BEGIN/END - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
RectBounds *LivingObjectPane::ComputeRenderBounds(RectBounds *outBounds)
{
    MapCoordinatePair screenPoint;
    RectBounds imageBounds;
    RectBounds renderBounds;

    g_pActiveMapPane->GetObjectScreenPoint(this, &screenPoint);

    if (m_objectStatus.m_statusKind != ObjectStatusBlob::HumanObject)
    {
        if (m_animationGroup < 9 && m_isMoving)
        {
            g_pMonsterImageLib->ComputeRenderBounds(
                &m_objectStatus,
                m_facing + 5,
                m_moveFrame,
                &imageBounds,
                &renderBounds);
        }
        else
        {
            g_pMonsterImageLib->ComputeRenderBounds(
                &m_objectStatus,
                m_animationGroup,
                m_animationFrame,
                &imageBounds,
                &renderBounds);
        }
    }
    else
    {
        g_pNewHumanImageLib->CalculateBounds(
            &m_objectStatus,
            m_facing,
            m_currentEffect,
            m_moveFrame,
            &imageBounds,
            &renderBounds,
            0,
            m_isMoving,
            m_boxRotationStep);
    }

    renderBounds.Offset(screenPoint.x, screenPoint.y);
    *outBounds = renderBounds;
    return outBounds;
}

class Motion;
class List;
class AttachedObjectPane;
class GrafPort;
struct NxRect;

class LivingObjectPane : public ObjectPane
{
public:
    enum TimerMessage
    {
        kLivingTimerForwardStep = 0x41645746,
        kLivingTimerBoxStep = 0x41534246,
        kLivingTimerDirectionDelay = 0x43684469,
        kLivingTimerFrameAnimation = 0x446e4146,
        kLivingTimerRefresh = 0x52654461
    };

    class BoxRotator : public FrameHandler
    {
    public:
        explicit BoxRotator(LivingObjectPane *owner) : m_owner(owner) {}
        virtual ~BoxRotator() {}

        virtual void Advance(int frame);
        void Start();
        void Stop();

    private:
        friend class LivingObjectPane;
        LivingObjectPane *m_owner;
    };

    LivingObjectPane(
        int objectId,
        int mapX,
        int mapY,
        const ObjectStatusBlob &objectStatus,
        unsigned char facing);
    LivingObjectPane(int objectId, int mapX, int mapY, unsigned char facing);
    virtual ~LivingObjectPane();

    void GetSpriteConfig(ObjectStatusBlob *out) const;
    bool SetSpriteConfig(const ObjectStatusBlob *objectStatus, unsigned char facing);
    void InitializeSpriteConfig(const ObjectStatusBlob *objectStatus, unsigned char facing);
    bool HasGMNameSuffix() const;

    void SetMovingState(bool moving);
    void SetLinkedGroundObject(ObjectPane *pane);
    void InsertType2AttachedObject(AttachedObjectPane *pane);
    void InsertType4AttachedObject(AttachedObjectPane *pane);
    void SetLinkedShadowObject(ObjectPane *pane);
    void SetLinkedEpfObject(ObjectPane *pane);
    void ReplaceLinkedNameplateObject(ObjectPane *pane);
    void DestroyLinkedObjects();
    void ClearAttachedObjects();

    void AddMotion(Motion *effect);
    void RemoveMotion(Motion *effect);

- Destination 10: UID0002R1 `by-memory/0x0053b700-0x0053baa8.LivingObjectPaneAnimationTimer.md`; replace its stale `LivingSpriteConfig`/embedded-handler aliases and descriptive callback name with this complete ObjectStatusBlob/inherited-TimerHandler block while preserving every source body and branch.

*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:BEGIN | ONLY MODIFY BETWEEN BEGIN/END - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
void LivingObjectPane::PlayAnimation(unsigned int animationGroup)
{
    if (m_objectStatus.m_statusKind != ObjectStatusBlob::HumanObject)
        return;

    CancelTimer(kLivingTimerFrameAnimation);

    const ImageFrameGroup *frameGroup =
        g_pObjectImageLib->GetFrameGroup(m_objectStatus.m_appearanceId - 0x8000, animationGroup);
    if (frameGroup->frameCount == 0)
    {
        if (animationGroup == 0)
            g_pActiveMapPane->RemoveObjectPaneById(m_objectId);
        return;
    }

    m_animationFrame = 0;
    m_animationGroup = animationGroup;

    const ImageFrame *frame = frameGroup->GetFrame(0);
    g_pTimerMgr->ScheduleTimer(
        static_cast<TimerHandler *>(this),
        kLivingTimerFrameAnimation,
        frame->delay,
        0,
        0);
    g_pActiveMapPane->InvalidateObject(this);
}

void LivingObjectPane::PlayFacingAnimation()
{
    PlayAnimation(m_facing + 1);
}

void LivingObjectPane::PlayDeathAnimation()
{
    m_terminalAnimation = true;
    PlayAnimation(0);
    g_pActiveMapPane->ClearLivingObjectReferences(this);
}

bool LivingObjectPane::OnTimer(int timerId, int arg0, int arg1)
{
    switch (timerId)
    {
    case kLivingTimerForwardStep:
        m_moveFrame = (m_moveFrame + 1) % 4;
        if (m_moveFrame != 0)
        {
            g_pTimerMgr->ScheduleTimer(
                static_cast<TimerHandler *>(this),
                kLivingTimerForwardStep,
                arg1,
                arg0,
                arg1);
            PropagateLinkedObjectMode(m_facing);
            return true;
        }

        TryStepForward();
        return true;

    case kLivingTimerBoxStep:
        m_boxRotationStep = (m_boxRotationStep + 1) % 6;
        g_pTimerMgr->ScheduleTimer(
            static_cast<TimerHandler *>(this),
            kLivingTimerBoxStep,
            200,
            0,
            0);
        g_pActiveMapPane->InvalidateObject(this);
        return true;

    case kLivingTimerDirectionDelay:
        if (m_isMoving)
        {
            g_pTimerMgr->ScheduleTimer(
                static_cast<TimerHandler *>(this),
                kLivingTimerDirectionDelay,
                m_objectStatus.m_displayStatusByte,
                arg0,
                0);
            return true;
        }

        m_facing = static_cast<unsigned char>(arg0);
        if (m_objectStatus.m_statusKind == ObjectStatusBlob::HumanObject)
            PlayAnimation(static_cast<unsigned char>(arg0) + 1);
        g_pActiveMapPane->InvalidateObject(this);
        return true;

    case kLivingTimerFrameAnimation:
    {
        const ImageFrameGroup *frameGroup = g_pObjectImageLib->GetFrameGroup(
            m_objectStatus.m_appearanceId - 0x8000,
            m_animationGroup);
        ++m_animationFrame;

        if (m_animationFrame == frameGroup->frameCount)
        {
            if (m_terminalAnimation)
                g_pActiveMapPane->RemoveObjectPaneById(m_objectId);
            else
            {
                m_animationFrame = 0;
                PlayAnimation(m_facing + 1);
            }
            return true;
        }

        const ImageFrame *frame = frameGroup->GetFrame(m_animationFrame);
        g_pTimerMgr->ScheduleTimer(
            static_cast<TimerHandler *>(this),
            kLivingTimerFrameAnimation,
            frame->delay,
            0,
            0);
        Invalidate(false);
        g_pActiveMapPane->InvalidateObject(this);
        return true;
    }

    case kLivingTimerRefresh:
        if (m_renderEffectState != 0)
        {
            m_renderEffectState = 0;
            Invalidate(&m_bounds);
        }
        return true;

    default:
        return false;
    }
}

int SendObjectActionPacket(unsigned char action, unsigned int objectId);

void UserPane::UseInventorySlot(unsigned char slot)
{
    if (slot == 0 || slot > 52)
        return;

    LocalInventorySlotRecord *record =
        GetInventorySlotAddress(static_cast<char>(slot));
    if (record == 0 || !record->active)
        return;

    if (record->targetMode == 0)
    {
        SendObjectActionPacket(slot, m_objectId);
        return;
    }

    if (record->targetMode == 1 &&
        g_activeMapPane->m_selectedObjectActive == 1 &&
        g_activeMapPane->m_selectedObjectPane != 0)
    {
        SendObjectActionPacket(
            slot, g_activeMapPane->m_selectedObjectPane->m_objectId);
        return;
    }

    ItemWhoInputPaneState *state =
        static_cast<ItemWhoInputPaneState *>(
            g_pGeneralPurposePanel->GetChildPaneByIndex(3));
    if (state->m_active)
        return;

    new ItemWhoInputPane(slot);

    state = static_cast<ItemWhoInputPaneState *>(
        g_pGeneralPurposePanel->GetChildPaneByIndex(3));
    state->SetInputModeFlag(true);
}

*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:[[[]]] | ONLY MODIFY VALUE - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:BEGIN | ONLY MODIFY BETWEEN BEGIN/END - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
void UserPane::UseCommandSlot(unsigned char slot)
{
    if (!m_spellCommandSlotsReady || slot == 0 || slot > 52)
        return;

    SpellCommandSlotRecord *record = GetSpellCommandSlotRecord(slot);
    if (record == 0 || !record->active)
        return;

    switch (record->inputKind)
    {
    case kSpellInputString:
        new SpellStringInputPane(slot);
        return;
    case kSpellInputTarget:
        if (g_activeMapPane != 0 && g_activeMapPane->HasPendingSpellTarget())
        {
            LivingObjectPane *target = g_activeMapPane->GetPendingSpellTarget();
            if (target != 0)
            {
                MapPosition firstPosition;
                MapPosition secondPosition;
                target->GetMapPosition(&firstPosition);
                target->GetMapPosition(&secondPosition);
                SendTargetedActionPacket(
                    slot, target->GetObjectId(),
                    secondPosition.word1, firstPosition.word0);
                return;
            }
        }

        new SpellWhoInputPane(slot);
        {
            LivingObjectPane *target = 0;
            if (g_activeMapPane != 0)
                target = g_activeMapPane->FindObjectPaneById(s_spellWhoTargetId);
            if (target == 0 || target->GetObjectType() != kObjectTypeLiving ||
                target->IsTargetSuppressed())
                target = g_pUserPane;
            if (target != 0)
            {
                target->SetTargetHighlight(true);
                s_spellWhoTargetId = target->GetObjectId();
            }
        }
        return;
    case kSpellInputFourNumbers:
        new SpellFourArgsInputPane(slot);
        return;
    case kSpellInputThreeNumbers:
        new SpellThreeArgsInputPane(slot);
        return;
    case kSpellInputNone:
        SendImmediateSpellCommand(slot);
        return;
    case kSpellInputTwoNumbers:
        new SpellTwoArgsInputPane(slot);
        return;
    case kSpellInputOneNumber:
        new SpellOneArgInputPane(slot);
        return;
    case kSpellInputSlot:
        new SpellSlotInputPane(slot);
        return;
    default:
        return;
    }
}

bool UserPane::WarpToPosition(
    int x, int y, int viewportColumn, int viewportRow)
{
    if (!m_mapPane->ValidateMapAndViewportCoords(
            x, y, viewportColumn, viewportRow))
        return false;

    if (m_tileX != x || m_tileY != y)
    {
        MapPoint oldPosition;
        MapPoint newPosition;
        InitMapPoint(&oldPosition, m_tileY, m_tileX);
        InitMapPoint(&newPosition, y, x);
        m_mapPane->ReindexObjectPanePosition(
            this, oldPosition.y, oldPosition.x,
            newPosition.y, newPosition.x);
        m_tileX = x;
        m_tileY = y;
    }

    m_viewportColumn = viewportColumn;
    m_viewportRow = viewportRow;
    CancelTimer(kLivingTimerForwardStep);
    CancelTimer(kUserPaneMovementStep);
    ClearAllEffects();
    m_isMoving = false;
    ClearMovementHistoryToken();
    SetDirectionDelayActive(false);
    return m_mapPane->InvalidateObject(this);
}

    if (recordIndex >= m_movementHistoryCapacity)
        return 0;
    LocalMovementHistoryRecord *record =
        &m_movementHistoryRecords[recordIndex];
    return record->active != 0 ? record : 0;
}

void UserPane::UpdateScreenPosition()
{
    MapCoordinatePair screenPos;
    GetMapPosition(&screenPos);
    m_mapPane->MapToScreenCoords(&screenPos);

    MapCoordinatePair stepOffset;
    m_mapPane->DirectionToTileOffset(&stepOffset, m_facing);
    ScaleDirectionOffsetToPixels(&stepOffset);

    const int divisor = 4 * g_movementSubstepScale;
    const int screenX = screenPos.x +
        (m_moveFrame * stepOffset.x) / divisor + g_mapTilePixelWidth / 2;
    const int screenY = screenPos.y +
        (m_moveFrame * stepOffset.y) / divisor + g_mapTilePixelHeight / 2;
    m_screenX = screenX;
    m_screenY = screenY;

    InitRectBounds(
        &m_screenHitBounds,
        screenX - m_localPlayerHitRadius,
        screenY - m_localPlayerHitRadius,
        screenX + m_localPlayerHitRadius,
        screenY + m_localPlayerHitRadius);
    InitRectBounds(
        &m_screenLowerHitBounds,
        screenX - m_localPlayerHitRadius,
        screenY,
        screenX + m_localPlayerHitRadius,
        screenY + m_localPlayerHitRadius);
}

*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:[[[]]] | ONLY MODIFY VALUE - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***

void UserPane::DispatchConfiguredShortcutHotkey(
    short hotkeyNumber, int unused)
{
    (void)unused;
    int shortcutIndex;
    if (hotkeyNumber == 0)
        shortcutIndex = 9;
    else if (hotkeyNumber == 10)
        shortcutIndex = 19;
    else
        shortcutIndex = hotkeyNumber - 1;

    const CompactShortcutRecord &shortcut =
        g_pConfig->m_userProfile.m_compactShortcutRecords[shortcutIndex];
    const wchar_t configuredKey = shortcut.key;
    if (!isalpha(static_cast<unsigned char>(configuredKey)))
        return;

    unsigned char slot = 0xff;
    if (configuredKey >= L'a' && configuredKey <= L'z')
        slot = static_cast<unsigned char>(configuredKey - L'a' + 1);
    else if (configuredKey >= L'A' && configuredKey <= L'Z')
        slot = static_cast<unsigned char>(configuredKey - L'A' + 27);

    if (shortcut.mode != 0)
        UseCommandSlot(slot);
    else
        UseInventorySlot(slot);
}

void UserPane::TryPerformAction()
{
    const unsigned char actionMode = m_actionMode;
    if (actionMode == 3 || actionMode == 1)
        return;

    unsigned int delay = 500;
    if (ClassifyHumanPartFiveBucket(m_objectStatus.m_weaponId) == 3)
        delay = 100;

    const unsigned int currentTick = g_pTimerMgr->currentTick;
    if (currentTick - m_lastActionTick <= delay)
        return;

    const int secondaryClass =
        ClassifyHumanPartTwoBucket(m_objectStatus.m_shieldOrArrowId);
    if (actionMode != 0 && secondaryClass != 2)
    {
        SystemMessagePane *pane = g_pChattingViewport;
        pane->m_pendingRefreshByte = 0;
        pane->AppendColoredText(kArrowShortageWarningText, 0x84, 0x24);
        pane->RefreshAndPruneMessages();
        return;
    }

    SendActionPacketWithAutoFace();
    if (actionMode == 1 || actionMode == 2)
        PlayActionAnimationOrEffect(27, 200, 0);
    m_lastActionTick = currentTick;
}

*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:[[[]]] | ONLY MODIFY VALUE - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:BEGIN | ONLY MODIFY BETWEEN BEGIN/END - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
bool UserPane::HandleChatMessagePacket(const unsigned char *packet)
{
    if (packet[1] != 0)
        return false;

    const unsigned int textByteCount = PacketBufferReadUInt16BE(packet + 2);
    if (textByteCount >= 0x8000)
        __report_rangecheckfailure();

    char ansiText[0x8000];
    wchar_t wideText[0x8000];
    wchar_t senderScratch[128];
    GetMemoryMan();
    MemmoveWrapper(ansiText, packet + 4, textByteCount);
    ansiText[textByteCount] = 0;

    const int wideCount = MultiByteToWideChar(
        CP_ACP, 0, ansiText, static_cast<int>(textByteCount),
        wideText, 0x8000);
    if (static_cast<unsigned int>(static_cast<unsigned short>(wideCount)) >=
        0x8000)
        __report_rangecheckfailure();
    wideText[static_cast<unsigned short>(wideCount)] = 0;

    if ((wideText[0] == L'-' && wideText[1] == L'>' && wideText[2] == L' ') ||
        wcsstr(wideText, L"> ") != 0)
        return false;

    wchar_t *quoteSpace = wcsstr(wideText, L"\" ");
    if (quoteSpace == 0)
        return false;
    *quoteSpace = 0;
    wcsncpy_s(senderScratch, 128, wideText, 128);
    senderScratch[35] = 0;
    if (wcschr(senderScratch, L' ') != 0)
        return false;

    List *senders = m_incomingWhisperSenderHistory;
    int foundIndex = -1;
    const int count = senders->GetCount();
    for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i)
    {
        const wchar_t *stored =
            static_cast<const wchar_t *>(senders->GetElementAt(i));
        if (wcscmp(stored, senderScratch) == 0)
        {
            foundIndex = i;
            break;
        }
    }

    if (foundIndex != -1)
        senders->RemoveAt(foundIndex, 1);
    else if (count >= 10)
        senders->RemoveAt(0, 1);
    senders->Append(1, senderScratch);
    m_whisperHistorySource = WhisperHistorySource_IncomingSender;
    return false;
}

*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:[[[]]] | ONLY MODIFY VALUE - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:BEGIN | ONLY MODIFY BETWEEN BEGIN/END - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
void UserPane::ProcessMovement(unsigned char direction)
{
    if (m_isMoving)
        return;

    if (m_playerSettingsState[3] == 1)
        direction = static_cast<unsigned char>((direction + 2) & 3);
    else if (m_playerSettingsState[3] != 0)
        direction = static_cast<unsigned char>(g_pTimerMgr->currentTick & 3);

    if (direction != m_facing)
    {
        SendDirectionPacket(direction);
        SetFacing(direction);
        return;
    }
    if (m_nameVisible)
        return;

    if (g_useEpfAssets)
    {
        g_pActiveMapPane->RequestObjectEffect(m_objectId, 201, 0, false);
        StopMatchingLoopingEffect(m_type4AttachedObjects, 201);
        StopMatchingLoopingEffect(m_type2AttachedObjects, 201);
    }

    MapPoint step;
    m_mapPane->DirectionToTileOffset(&step, direction);
    const int nextX = m_tileX + step.x;
    const int nextY = m_tileY + step.y;
    const bool movementBlocked =
        m_mapPane->IsMovementOutsideViewport(
            nextX, nextY, m_viewportColumn, m_viewportRow,
            m_movementLimitTop, m_movementLimitLeft,
            m_movementLimitBottom, m_movementLimitRight);
    const int movementResult =
        m_mapPane->ResolveLocalMovement(m_tileX, m_tileY, direction);

    if (movementBlocked || movementResult == 0)
    {
        unsigned char blockedPacket[7];
        blockedPacket[0] = 0x69;
        PacketBufferWriteUInt16BE(blockedPacket + 1, m_tileX);
        PacketBufferWriteUInt16BE(blockedPacket + 3, m_tileY);
        blockedPacket[5] = direction;
        blockedPacket[6] = 0;
        g_packetSender->QueueAndSendPacket(blockedPacket, 6);
        return;
    }

    if (movementResult > 0)
    {
        MapMovementArea area;
        unsigned short terrainToken =
            m_mapPane->GetMovementArea(direction, &area);
        unsigned char packet[0x12];
        int packetSize = 0;
        packet[packetSize++] = 0x06;
        packet[packetSize++] = direction;
        packet[packetSize++] = m_mapPane->AllowsMovementStatusFlag() &&
            g_pConfig->m_sendMovementStatusBit
                ? static_cast<unsigned char>(m_movementHistoryWriteIndex | 0x80)
                : static_cast<unsigned char>(m_movementHistoryWriteIndex & 0x7f);
        packet[packetSize++] = m_objectStatus.m_movementSpeed;
        PacketBufferAppendUInt16BE(packet, &packetSize, m_tileX);
        PacketBufferAppendUInt16BE(packet, &packetSize, m_tileY);
        PacketBufferAppendUInt16BE(packet, &packetSize, area.left);
        PacketBufferAppendUInt16BE(packet, &packetSize, area.top);
        PacketBufferAppendUInt8(packet, &packetSize, area.right - area.left);
        PacketBufferAppendUInt8(packet, &packetSize, area.bottom - area.top);
        PacketBufferAppendUInt16BE(packet, &packetSize, terrainToken);

        LocalMovementHistoryRecord &record =
            m_movementHistoryRecords[m_movementHistoryWriteIndex];
        record.active = 1;
        record.tileY = m_tileY;
        record.tileX = m_tileX;
        record.direction = direction;
        m_movementHistoryWriteIndex = static_cast<unsigned char>(
            (m_movementHistoryWriteIndex + 1) % m_movementHistoryCapacity);
        g_packetSender->QueueAndSendPacket(packet, packetSize);
        InitializeWalkAnimation(direction);

        if (m_mapPane->AllowsMovementStatusFlag() &&
            g_pConfig->m_sendMovementStatusBit)
            m_mapPane->SendMovementViewportState(
                0x0b, direction, m_tileX, m_tileY,
                m_viewportColumn, m_viewportRow);
        return;
    }

    SendFaceDirectionPacket(direction);
    StartDirectionalAnimation(direction);
    if (m_mapPane->AllowsMovementStatusFlag() &&
        g_pConfig->m_sendMovementStatusBit)
        m_mapPane->SendMovementViewportState(
            0x26, direction, m_tileX, m_tileY,
            m_viewportColumn, m_viewportRow);
}

void UserPane::StartDirectionalAnimation(unsigned char direction)
{
    m_isMoving = true;
    ClearMovementHistoryToken();
    SetDirectionDelayActive(false);
    m_moveFrame = 0;

    int frameDelay;
    if (m_objectStatus.m_statusKind == ObjectStatusBlob::HumanObject)
    {
        m_animationGroup = direction + 5;
        const ImageFrameGroup *group =
            g_pObjectImageLib->GetFrameGroup(
                m_objectStatus.m_appearanceId - 0x8000,
                m_animationGroup);
        frameDelay = group->GetFrame(m_moveFrame)->delay;
        PlayFacingAnimation();
        CancelBoxRotationStep();
    }
    else
    {
        frameDelay =
            m_objectStatus.m_movementSpeed / g_movementSubstepScale;
    }

    SetMovingState(true);
    g_pTimerMgr->ScheduleTimer(
        static_cast<TimerHandler *>(this), kUserPaneMovementStep,
        frameDelay, direction, 0);
    m_mapPane->InvalidateObject(this);
}

*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:[[[]]] | ONLY MODIFY VALUE - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:BEGIN | ONLY MODIFY BETWEEN BEGIN/END - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
void UserPane::ExecuteHotkeyAction(short hotkey)
{
    const int index = (hotkey % 10 == 0) ? hotkey + 9 : hotkey - 1;
    const MacroHotkeyRecord &record =
        g_pConfig->m_userProfile.m_macroHotkeyRecords[index];

    if (record.mode == 1)
    {
        char text[256];
        unsigned char packet[300];
        const int textLength = WideCharToMultiByte(
            CP_ACP, 0, record.text, static_cast<int>(wcslen(record.text)),
            text, 256, 0, 0);
        if (static_cast<unsigned int>(textLength) >= 256)
            __report_rangecheckfailure();
        packet[0] = 0x0e;
        packet[1] = 0;
        packet[2] = static_cast<unsigned char>(textLength);
        memmove(packet + 3, text, textLength);
        g_packetSender->QueueAndSendPacket(packet, textLength + 3);
        return;
    }

    const wchar_t key = record.text[0];
    if (!isalpha(static_cast<unsigned char>(key)))
        return;
    unsigned char slot = 0xff;
    if (key >= L'a' && key <= L'z')
        slot = static_cast<unsigned char>(key - L'a' + 1);
    else if (key >= L'A' && key <= L'Z')
        slot = static_cast<unsigned char>(key - L'A' + 27);
    if (record.mode == 2)
        UseCommandSlot(slot);
    else if (record.mode == 3)
        UseInventorySlot(slot);
}

*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:[[[]]] | ONLY MODIFY VALUE - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:BEGIN | ONLY MODIFY BETWEEN BEGIN/END - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
void UserPane::SendMovementPacket(unsigned char direction)
{
    m_lastMovementInputTick = g_pTimerMgr->currentTick;
    std::vector<unsigned int> path;
    g_pActiveMapPane->BuildLocalMovementPath(path);
    g_pActiveMapPane->CompactMovementPath(path);

    unsigned char packet[60];
    unsigned int packetSize = 0;
    packet[packetSize++] = 0x13;
    if (direction != 0 || path.empty())
    {
        packet[packetSize++] = 4;
    }
    else
    {
        packet[packetSize++] =
            static_cast<unsigned char>(2 + (m_actionMode == 2));
        unsigned int elapsed =
            (m_lastMovementInputTick - m_lastMovementSendTick) / 125;
        if (elapsed > 40)
            elapsed = 40;
        packet[packetSize++] = static_cast<unsigned char>(elapsed);
        packet[packetSize++] = static_cast<unsigned char>(path.size());
        for (size_t i = 0; i < path.size() && i < 9; ++i)
            PacketBufferAppendUInt32BE(packet, &packetSize, path[i]);
    }
    if (packetSize >= 60)
        __report_rangecheckfailure();
    g_packetSender->QueueAndSendPacket(packet, packetSize);
}

void UserPane::TryDispatchFunctionKeyShortcut(
    unsigned short functionKeyOrdinal, unsigned int currentTick)
{
    if (currentTick >= g_lastFunctionKeyTick &&
        currentTick - g_lastFunctionKeyTick <= 100)
        return;

    const unsigned int row = functionKeyOrdinal == 0
        ? 9 : static_cast<unsigned int>(functionKeyOrdinal - 1);
    const wchar_t key = g_pConfig->m_userProfile.m_legacyShortcutText[row][0];
    unsigned char slot = 0xff;
    if (key >= L'a' && key <= L'z')
        slot = static_cast<unsigned char>(key - L'a' + 1);
    else if (key >= L'A' && key <= L'Z')
        slot = static_cast<unsigned char>(key - L'A' + 27);
    UseCommandSlot(slot);
}

*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:[[[]]] | ONLY MODIFY VALUE - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:BEGIN | ONLY MODIFY BETWEEN BEGIN/END - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
bool UserPane::ParsePlayerSettingsPacket(const unsigned char *packet)
{
    const unsigned char flags = packet[1];
    m_playerSettingsState[0] = static_cast<unsigned char>(flags >> 7);
    if (flags & 0x04)
        m_objectStatus.m_actionState = 3;
    m_playerSettingsState[2] = (flags & 0x02) == 0;

    unsigned int offset = (flags & 0x40) ? 39 : 10;
    if (flags & 0x20)
        offset += 8;
    if (flags & 0x10)
        offset += 9;
    if (flags & 0x08)
    {
        const unsigned char oldSpeed = m_playerSettingsState[4];
        m_playerSettingsState[3] = packet[offset++];
        m_playerSettingsState[4] = packet[offset++];
        m_playerSettingsState[5] = packet[offset++];
        m_playerSettingsState[6] = packet[offset++];
        ++offset;
        if (oldSpeed != m_playerSettingsState[4])
            m_mapPane->RefreshMovementBounds();
    }

    g_pConfig->m_sendMovementStatusBit = packet[++offset];
    if (g_pConfig->m_sendMovementStatusBit && IsDirectionDelayActive())
    {
        RemovePendingTimers();
        SetDirectionDelayActive(false);
        m_isMoving = false;
    }

    const unsigned short options =
        PacketBufferReadUInt16BE(packet + offset + 1);
    g_pConfig->ApplyServerOptionBits(options);
    if (g_pConfig->m_soundEnabled)
        g_pSoundManager->EnableSound();
    else
        g_pSoundManager->DisableSound();
    g_pMainWindow->RefreshSettingsPaneIfVisible();
    return false;
}

*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:[[[]]] | ONLY MODIFY VALUE - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:BEGIN | ONLY MODIFY BETWEEN BEGIN/END - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
bool UserPane::ParseInventorySlotUpdatePacket(const unsigned char *packet)
{
    unsigned int offset = 1;
    const unsigned char slot = packet[offset++];
    const unsigned short itemId = PacketBufferReadUInt16BE(packet + offset);
    offset += 2;
    const unsigned char iconStyle = packet[offset++];

    wchar_t displayName[256];
    wchar_t secondaryText[256];
    wchar_t tertiaryText[256];
    ReadLengthPrefixedAnsiText(packet, &offset, displayName, 256);
    ReadLengthPrefixedAnsiText(packet, &offset, secondaryText, 256);
    const unsigned int quantity = PacketBufferReadUInt32BE(packet, &offset);
    const bool quantityPrompt = PacketBufferReadUInt8(packet, &offset) != 0;
    const unsigned int value1f0 = PacketBufferReadUInt32BE(packet, &offset);
    const unsigned int value1f4 = PacketBufferReadUInt8(packet, &offset);
    ReadLengthPrefixedAnsiText(packet, &offset, tertiaryText, 256);
    const bool targetMode = PacketBufferReadUInt8(packet, &offset) != 0;

    if (slot >= 1 && slot <= g_pCollectionData->m_inventorySlotCount)
    {
        LocalInventorySlotRecord &record = m_inventorySlots[slot - 1];
        record.active = 1;
        record.itemId = itemId;
        record.iconStyle = iconStyle;
        wcscpy_s(record.displayName, 80, displayName);
        wcscpy_s(record.secondaryText, 80, secondaryText);
        wcscpy_s(record.tertiaryText, 80, tertiaryText);
        record.quantityOrCount = quantity;
        record.quantityPromptFlag = quantityPrompt ? 1 : 0;
        record.value1f0 = value1f0;
        record.value1f4 = value1f4;
        record.targetMode = targetMode ? 1 : 0;
    }
    return false;
}

*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:[[[]]] | ONLY MODIFY VALUE - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:BEGIN | ONLY MODIFY BETWEEN BEGIN/END - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
bool UserPane::HandleServerEntryPacket(const unsigned char *packet)
{
    g_pMainWindow->ClosePaneByRole(MainWindowPane_Login);
    if (g_useEpfAssets)
        g_pTransferDialog->ResetForServerEntry();
    g_packetSender->ResetReceiveState();
    if (g_pLoadingPane != 0)
    {
        g_pLoadingPane->Close();
        g_pApplication->DeleteObject(g_pLoadingPane);
    }

    m_objectId = PacketBufferReadUInt32BE(packet + 1);
    m_facing = packet[8];
    const unsigned char serverMode = packet[9];
    if (serverMode <= 4)
    {
        if (g_useEpfAssets)
            g_pCollectionData->SetServerMode(serverMode);
        else
            g_pLegacyCollectionData->SetServerMode(serverMode);
    }
    m_mapPane->InvalidateObject(this);

    const ServerAddressList &servers =
        g_pConfig->m_userProfile.m_serverAddresses;
    if (servers.size() < 255)
    {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < servers.size(); ++i)
        {
            if (servers[i].active)
                continue;
            unsigned char request[256];
            int requestSize = 0;
            PacketBufferAppendUInt8(request, &requestSize, 0x0d);
            PacketBufferAppendUInt8(request, &requestSize, 2);
            PacketBufferAppendAnsiString(
                request, &requestSize, servers[i].address);
            g_packetSender->QueueAndSendPacket(request, requestSize);
        }
    }
    return true;
}

bool UserPane::HandleMovementReplyPacket(const unsigned char *packet)
{
    const unsigned char direction = packet[1];
    const int serverX = PacketBufferReadUInt16BE(packet + 2);
    const int serverY = PacketBufferReadUInt16BE(packet + 4);
    const int viewportColumn = PacketBufferReadUInt16BE(packet + 6);
    const int viewportRow = PacketBufferReadUInt16BE(packet + 8);
    const unsigned char historyIndex = packet[10] & 0x7f;

    if (!m_isMoving &&
        (!m_mapPane->AllowsMovementStatusFlag() ||
         !g_pConfig->m_sendMovementStatusBit))
    {
        WarpToPosition(serverX, serverY, viewportColumn, viewportRow);
        if (direction != 4)
            InitializeWalkAnimation(direction);
        SetMovementReplyState();
        m_mapPane->RecenterAndSendPosition(
            serverX, serverY, viewportColumn, viewportRow, false);
        m_mapPane->InvalidateObject(this);
        RefreshAllLinkedObjects();
        g_pTimerMgr->RemoveTimersForOwner(&m_mapPane->m_timerHandler);
        return true;
    }

    if (!m_mapPane->AllowsMovementStatusFlag() ||
        g_pConfig->m_sendMovementStatusBit != 1)
    {
        if (serverX == m_tileX && serverY == m_tileY && direction == m_facing)
        {
            SetMovementReplyState();
            if (IsDirectionDelayActive())
            {
                const int eventId = m_actionMode != 0
                    ? kLivingTimerForwardStep : kUserPaneMovementStep;
                g_pTimerMgr->ScheduleTimer(
                    static_cast<TimerHandler *>(this), eventId,
                    0, m_facing, 0);
            }
            return false;
        }

        MapPoint step;
        m_mapPane->DirectionToTileOffset(&step, direction);
        WarpToPosition(
            serverX + step.x, serverY + step.y,
            viewportColumn, viewportRow);
        m_mapPane->RecenterAndSendPosition(
            serverX + step.x, serverY + step.y,
            viewportColumn, viewportRow, false);
        m_mapPane->InvalidateObject(this);
        RefreshAllLinkedObjects();
        g_pTimerMgr->RemoveTimersForOwner(&m_mapPane->m_timerHandler);
        return false;
    }

    if (direction & 0x80)
    {
        SetMovementReplyState();
        if (IsDirectionDelayActive())
            g_pTimerMgr->ScheduleTimer(
                static_cast<TimerHandler *>(this),
                kLivingTimerForwardStep, 0, m_facing, 0);
        return false;
    }
    if (historyIndex >= m_movementHistoryCapacity)
        return false;
    LocalMovementHistoryRecord &record =
        m_movementHistoryRecords[historyIndex];
    if (!record.active)
        return false;
    if (record.tileX != serverX || record.tileY != serverY ||
        record.direction != direction)
    {
        MapPoint step;
        m_mapPane->DirectionToTileOffset(&step, direction);
        WarpToPosition(
            serverX + step.x, serverY + step.y,
            viewportColumn, viewportRow);
        m_mapPane->RecenterAndSendPosition(
            serverX + step.x, serverY + step.y,
            viewportColumn, viewportRow, false);
        m_mapPane->InvalidateObject(this);
        RefreshAllLinkedObjects();
        g_pTimerMgr->RemoveTimersForOwner(&m_mapPane->m_timerHandler);
        ClearAllMovementHistoryRecords();
    }
    ClearMovementHistoryRecordByIndex(historyIndex);
    return false;
}
*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:END | DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***

*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:BEGIN | ONLY MODIFY BETWEEN BEGIN/END - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
bool UserPane::HandleUserListPacket(const ServerMessageEvent *event)
{
    if (m_deferredUserListActive)
    {
        m_deferredUserListActive = false;
    }
    else
    {
        const unsigned char *packet = event->packetData;
        const size_t packetSize = event->packetSize;
        const unsigned short entryCount =
            PacketBufferReadUInt16BE(packet + 3);
        m_deferredUserListCount = g_pTimerMgr->currentTick;
        if (entryCount < 100)
        {
            m_deferredUserListDeadline = m_deferredUserListCount;
        }
        else if (entryCount < 300)
        {
            m_deferredUserListDeadline = m_deferredUserListCount + 30000;
            CopyDeferredUserListPacket(packet, packetSize);
        }
        else if (entryCount < 500)
        {
            m_deferredUserListDeadline = m_deferredUserListCount + 60000;
            CopyDeferredUserListPacket(packet, packetSize);
        }
        else if (entryCount < 800)
        {
            m_deferredUserListDeadline = m_deferredUserListCount + 90000;
            CopyDeferredUserListPacket(packet, packetSize);
        }
        else
        {
            m_deferredUserListDeadline = m_deferredUserListCount + 150000;
            free(m_deferredUserListPacket);
            m_deferredUserListPacket =
                static_cast<unsigned char *>(malloc(packetSize));
            m_deferredUserListPacketSize = packetSize;
            memmove(m_deferredUserListPacket, packet, packetSize);
        }
    }

    if (g_pUserListDialogPane == 0)
        new UserListDialogPane(event->packetData);
    return true;
}

static bool __stdcall HandleUIPanelSwitchPacket(const unsigned char *packet)
{
    unsigned int offset = 1;
    const unsigned char panel = PacketBufferReadUInt8(packet, &offset);
    if (g_useEpfAssets)
    {
        switch (panel)
        {
        case 0: g_pMainWindow->SwitchAuxiliaryPanel(2, false); break;
        case 2: g_pMainWindow->SwitchAuxiliaryPanel(3, false); break;
        case 4:
            RefreshUserPanelState();
            g_pMainWindow->GetPaneByRole(MainWindowPane_User)->m_panelMode = 0;
            break;
        case 6: OpenCollectionPanel(); break;
        case 7:
            RefreshUserPanelState();
            g_pMainWindow->GetPaneByRole(MainWindowPane_User)->m_panelMode = 3;
            break;
        case 8:
            RefreshUserPanelState();
            g_pMainWindow->GetPaneByRole(MainWindowPane_User)->m_panelMode = 2;
            break;
        }
    }
    else
    {
        switch (panel)
        {
        case 0: g_pMainWindow->SwitchAuxiliaryPanel(2, false); break;
        case 2: g_pMainWindow->SwitchAuxiliaryPanel(3, false); break;
        case 4:
            RefreshUserPanelState();
            g_pMainWindow->GetPaneByRole(MainWindowPane_User)->m_panelMode = 1;
            break;
        case 6: OpenCollectionPanel(); break;
        case 7:
            RefreshUserPanelState();
            g_pMainWindow->GetPaneByRole(MainWindowPane_User)->m_panelMode = 3;
            break;
        case 8:
            RefreshUserPanelState();
            g_pMainWindow->GetPaneByRole(MainWindowPane_User)->m_panelMode = 2;
            break;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

bool UserPane::ParseLinkedObjectsPacket(const unsigned char *packet)
{
    const signed char newCount = static_cast<signed char>(packet[0]);
    if (m_linkedPlayerCount > newCount)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < m_linkedPlayerCount; ++i)
            g_pActiveMapPane->RemoveLinkedPlayer(m_linkedPlayers[i].objectId);
    }
    m_linkedPlayerCount = newCount;

    unsigned int offset = 1;
    for (int i = 0; i < m_linkedPlayerCount; ++i)
    {
        LinkedPlayerRecord &record = m_linkedPlayers[i];
        record.objectId = PacketBufferReadUInt32BE(packet + offset);
        offset += 4;

        const unsigned int nameLength = packet[offset++];
        if (nameLength >= 128)
            __report_rangecheckfailure();
        char name[128];
        memmove(name, packet + offset, nameLength);
        offset += nameLength;
        name[nameLength] = 0;
        const int wideLength = MultiByteToWideChar(
            CP_ACP, 0, name, nameLength, record.name, 128);
        record.name[wideLength] = 0;

        record.active = packet[offset++] != 0;
        record.field106 = PacketBufferReadUInt16BE(packet + offset);
        offset += 2;
        record.state108[0] = packet[offset++];
        record.state108[2] = packet[offset++];
        record.state108[1] = packet[offset++];
        record.state108[3] = packet[offset++];
        record.state108[4] = packet[offset++];
        record.state108[5] = packet[offset++];
        record.state108[6] = packet[offset++];
        record.field110 = PacketBufferReadUInt16BE(packet + offset);
        offset += 2;
        record.field112 = packet[offset++];
        record.field114 = PacketBufferReadUInt16BE(packet + offset);
        offset += 2;
        record.field116 = packet[offset++];
        record.field118 = PacketBufferReadUInt16BE(packet + offset);
        offset += 2;
        record.field11a = packet[offset++];
        record.value11c = PacketBufferReadUInt32BE(packet + offset); offset += 4;
        record.value120 = PacketBufferReadUInt32BE(packet + offset); offset += 4;
        record.value124 = PacketBufferReadUInt32BE(packet + offset); offset += 4;
        record.value128 = PacketBufferReadUInt32BE(packet + offset); offset += 4;
        g_pActiveMapPane->RefreshLinkedPlayer(record.objectId);
    }
    return true;
}
