*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:[[[]]] | ONLY MODIFY VALUE - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:BEGIN | ONLY MODIFY BETWEEN BEGIN/END - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
void UserPane::SendMovementWaypointPacket(
    unsigned char direction, unsigned short token,
    const MovementWaypointSegment *segment)
{
    unsigned char packet[0x12];
    int packetSize = 0;
    packet[packetSize++] = 0x06;
    packet[packetSize++] = direction;
    packet[packetSize++] = m_mapPane->AllowsMovementStatusFlag() &&
        g_pConfig->m_sendMovementStatusBit
            ? static_cast<unsigned char>(m_movementHistoryWriteIndex | 0x80)
            : static_cast<unsigned char>(m_movementHistoryWriteIndex & 0x7f);
    packet[packetSize++] = m_facing;

    MapPoint position;
    GetMapPosition(&position);
    PacketBufferAppendUInt16BE(packet, &packetSize, position.x);
    PacketBufferAppendUInt16BE(packet, &packetSize, position.y);
    PacketBufferAppendUInt16BE(packet, &packetSize, segment->startX);
    PacketBufferAppendUInt16BE(packet, &packetSize, segment->startY);
    PacketBufferAppendUInt8(
        packet, &packetSize, segment->endX - segment->startX);
    PacketBufferAppendUInt8(
        packet, &packetSize, segment->endY - segment->startY);
    PacketBufferAppendUInt16BE(packet, &packetSize, token);

    LocalMovementHistoryRecord &record =
        m_movementHistoryRecords[m_movementHistoryWriteIndex];
    record.active = 1;
    record.tileY = position.y;
    record.tileX = position.x;
    record.direction = direction;
    m_movementHistoryWriteIndex = static_cast<unsigned char>(
        (m_movementHistoryWriteIndex + 1) % m_movementHistoryCapacity);
    if (packetSize >= 0x12)
        __report_rangecheckfailure();
    g_packetSender->QueueAndSendPacket(packet, packetSize);
}

*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:[[[]]] | ONLY MODIFY VALUE - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:BEGIN | ONLY MODIFY BETWEEN BEGIN/END - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
void UserPane::SendAutoFaceDirectionPacket()
{
    if (m_actionMode != 1)
        return;
    ObjectPane *target =
        g_pActiveMapPane->FindObjectPaneById(g_selectedTargetObjectId);
    if (target == 0)
        return;

    MapPoint targetPosition;
    target->GetMapPosition(&targetPosition);
    const int deltaX = targetPosition.x - m_tileX;
    const int deltaY = targetPosition.y - m_tileY;
    unsigned char direction;
    if (deltaY == 0)
        direction = deltaX <= 0 ? 3 : 1;
    else if (deltaX == 0)
        direction = deltaY <= 0 ? 0 : 2;
    else
    {
        const float slope = -static_cast<float>(deltaY) /
            static_cast<float>(deltaX);
        if (deltaX <= 0)
            direction = slope > 1.0f ? 2 : (slope < -1.0f ? 0 : 3);
        else
            direction = slope > 1.0f ? 0 : (slope < -1.0f ? 2 : 1);
    }
    if (direction != m_facing)
        SendDirectionPacket(direction);
}

*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:[[[]]] | ONLY MODIFY VALUE - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:BEGIN | ONLY MODIFY BETWEEN BEGIN/END - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
void UserPane::SendActionPacketWithAutoFace()
{
    unsigned char packet[3] = {0x13, 0, 0};
    int packetSize = 1;
    if (m_actionMode == 1)
    {
        ObjectPane *target =
            g_pActiveMapPane->FindObjectPaneById(g_selectedTargetObjectId);
        if (target != 0)
        {
            MapPoint targetPosition;
            target->GetMapPosition(&targetPosition);
            const int deltaX = targetPosition.x - m_tileX;
            const int deltaY = targetPosition.y - m_tileY;
            unsigned char direction;
            if (deltaY == 0)
                direction = deltaX <= 0 ? 3 : 1;
            else if (deltaX == 0)
                direction = deltaY <= 0 ? 0 : 2;
            else
            {
                const float slope = -static_cast<float>(deltaY) /
                    static_cast<float>(deltaX);
                if (deltaX <= 0)
                    direction = slope > 1.0f ? 2
                        : (slope < -1.0f ? 0 : 3);
                else
                    direction = slope > 1.0f ? 0
                        : (slope < -1.0f ? 2 : 1);
            }
            if (direction != m_facing)
                SendDirectionPacket(direction);
        }
        packet[1] = 1;
        packetSize = 2;
    }
    else if (m_actionMode == 2)
    {
        packet[1] = 1;
        packetSize = 2;
    }
    else if (m_actionMode == 0)
    {
        packet[1] = 0;
        packetSize = 2;
    }
    g_packetSender->QueueAndSendPacket(packet, packetSize);
}
*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:END | DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***

*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:[[[]]] | ONLY MODIFY VALUE - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
*** RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE:BEGIN | ONLY MODIFY BETWEEN BEGIN/END - DO NOT REMOVE!!! ***
void UserPane::SendAttackTargetPacket(unsigned char action)
{
    if (m_isMoving)
        return;
    NxRect targetRect;
    const unsigned short targetId =
        m_mapPane->GetAttackTargetRect(action, &targetRect);
    unsigned char packet[13];
    packet[0] = 0x82;
    packet[1] = action;
    packet[2] = static_cast<unsigned char>(m_viewportColumn);
    packet[3] = static_cast<unsigned char>(m_viewportRow);
    PacketBufferWriteUInt16BE(packet + 4, targetRect.left);
    PacketBufferWriteUInt16BE(packet + 6, targetRect.top);
    packet[8] = static_cast<unsigned char>(targetRect.right - targetRect.left);
    packet[9] = static_cast<unsigned char>(targetRect.bottom - targetRect.top);
    PacketBufferWriteUInt16BE(packet + 10, targetId);
    packet[12] = 0;
    g_packetSender->QueueAndSendPacket(packet, 12);
}
